### >>> Kernel PAC emulation, xnu 1019 (branch `claude/peaceful-saha-28595a` off develop, 2026-10-26): tools/pacemu/README.md <<<
* **What:** `xnu/patches-12377/1019-sweet-pac-emulation.patch` (number checked free on develop and `claude/wlan26-chip-037a03`, whose patches are 11xx). Same hook as 0141 (`handle_uncategorized`, `sweet_arm64e_enabled()`, EL0 64-bit): RETAA/RETAB -> RET, BRA* -> BR Xn, BLRA* -> BLR Xn (LR = PC+4, target read first so BLRAAZ X30 works), LDRAA/LDRAB plain + pre-index (simm10*8, SP base, load through `copyin` of the TBI-stripped address; a failing load raises EXC_BAD_ACCESS/KERN_INVALID_ADDRESS = SIGSEGV with the EL0 data-abort ESR, PC not advanced), PAC*/AUT*/XPAC* -> NOP, **PACGA = pacstrip's `EOR Xd,Xn,Xm,ROR #13` (Xm=SP: `MOV Xd,Xn`)**: deliberately the same function as the static rewrite (one PACGA in the whole system, so a tag made in stripped code verifies in unstripped code and the reverse; register-independent as `_NSThreadData` needs). Strict Arm ARM decode (Z forms need the 11111 field; pacstrip is lenient there). v8.9 forms and ERETA* not handled. Unknown words: SIGILL as before. Log: first 4 traps per class, then at every power of two from 1024, with process name/pid = the list of binaries worth stripping. Boot-arg: none new (`sweetarm64e=1` is already in mkboot-full.sh's args).
* **Checked on the Mac (observed):** `python3 tools/pacemu/check.py` PASS: unit tests; 43 forms + 25 neighbours in `forms.s` assembled with `clang -arch arm64e`; 22,191 fuzzed words vs `llvm-objdump --mattr=+pauth`, 0 mismatches; 751 emulated words give the same state as their pacstrip rewrite (74 unallocated Z-form words only pacstrip rewrites, 611 LDRAx offsets only the kernel handles); four decoder mutations all caught. sleh.c with 1019 syntax-checks clean with the local xnu-12377 build flags (research/xnu-local compile_commands.json); the full ordered series (dxb 26.0 + ours) applies with 1019 (same pre-existing local skips: link_kdk, remove_applefeatures, 0031/0041/0155 need the .sh stage scripts).
* **Profile (host, observed):** decode+execute ~1 ns; the first version's 264-byte register snapshot/compare cost ~13 ns more -> replaced by a written-register mask (`cpu.written`), software part now ~4 ns/trap on M-series. The trap itself (~1 us, to be measured by pactest's `PROF` lines vs a getppid syscall) dominates. Run-ahead (emulate the next PAC word in the same trap) is useless: in 300 macOS arm64e binaries 0 of 142,950 PAC words are adjacent; interpreting the MOV/LDR/ADD in between would save at most ~8 % of traps -> not done. The real fix for hot code stays stripping it (below).
* **Device tool:** `tools/pacemu/build.sh` -> `tools/pacemu/out/pactest` (arm64e, ad-hoc + tools/ssh-style entitlements, **no** renegade strip; census shows every class). PASS/FAIL per class + `PROF` ns per class. As a plain arm64 Mac build it passes everything that macOS can test (asm control flow/LR/loads; PACGA skipped: macOS keeps PAC keys off in arm64 processes).
* **Installer (user, mid-task: "replace PAC instructions with the non-PAC ones, e.g. for App Store apps"):** `tools/sweet-install-ipa.sh` now finds every Mach-O in the bundle, refuses any FairPlay-encrypted one (encrypted text cannot run here nor be rewritten), runs pacstrip on all of them before the ad-hoc re-sign. Dry-run on a fake IPA (two unstripped arm64e Mach-Os, a path with a space, a non-Mach-O) OK; **not run against the phone**. App Store downloads themselves stay unsupported (FairPlay; fairplayd is disabled).
* **CI (user OK 2026-10-26):** branch pushed; `gh workflow run xnu.yml -R ry-diffusion/Bergamota --ref claude/peaceful-saha-28595a -f macos_version=26.5` = run **40412147688: SUCCESS** (log: `Applying patch: .../sweet-1019-sweet-pac-emulation.patch`; no sleh.c/sweet_pacemu.h warnings beyond xnu's stock -Wreserved-identifier noise; errors.txt empty; link-errors.txt = host-tool dtrace libelf x86_64 ld warnings only). Artifact `xnu-kernel`: `kernel.development.sweet` sha256 ee1b98a15ed56352..., contains `SWEET: emulating %s 0x%08x at pc=0x%llx in %s[%d] (%llu so far)`. No boot image built yet. **NOT done:** no boot (user: "no boot yet"), pactest not run on the phone. Next: boot image with 1019 through the Bergamota queue with the user's OK (can ride along with a Wi-Fi/GPU boot), then `scp -O tools/pacemu/out/pactest root@192.168.7.2:/var/root/` and run it; read the `SWEET: emulating` lines to see which unstripped binaries trap.

### >>> Chlorophyll on Turnip (Chlorophyll branch `turnip` 6a27a3b, Bergamota branch `claude/chlorophyll-turnip-c432e2`), 2026-10-26: Turnip RUNS on the A630 <<<
* **INSTALLED SYSTEM-WIDE (2026-10-26 ~15:00-15:25 phone time, boot 51, user: "install libchlorophyll + the inject for backboardd", then "kill the dead, move everything to the new turnip one").** Chlorophyll `turnip` 7d34aac.
  * `/usr/local/lib/bgmtl/` now holds only `bgmtl-inject.dylib` (built from chl), `libchlorophyll.dylib` and the new `libdrm.2.dylib`. **Gallium/libbgair is removed from the phone.**
    * Backups: `/var/root/bgmtl-gallium-2026-10-26/` (inject 8c7a6d0e, libbgair 25ccfc5d, libdrm 90f707f5) and on the Mac at `work-chlorophyll/phone-backup-gallium-2026-10-26/`.
    * Revert: copy the three files back into `/usr/local/lib/bgmtl/` (temp name + mv), then remove `libchlorophyll.dylib`.
  * The same inject file is loaded by backboardd, iconservicesagent and Mercury (their own load command) and by SpringBoard and PosterBoard (`/usr/local/bgi.dylib` symlink). So every Chlorophyll process takes chl at its next start.
  * **backboardd is still running the old libbgair** it mapped at boot. It moves to chl at the next boot. That needs the user's OK; do not restart it by hand.
  * **Bugs found by SpringBoard on chl:**
    * RenderBox's uber fragments (`uber_vertex + fa16*mha4`) took 7 s each in ir3, and drawing them hung the GPU (hang 1). Cause: the frontend inlined a callee's early `return` without `nir_lower_returns`. Fixed in 7d34aac: they now build in about 3.5 s cold and SpringBoard drew them for a couple of minutes, about 50-75 fps while animating, with no failures.
    * Scratch (private memory) on Turnip checked with the new `bg_scratch`: `chl_test` 30/30.
  * **Hang 2 (cause unknown):** it came right after I killed the old Gallium iconservicesagent and a PosterBoard. SpringBoard's and Mercury's dead-man switches tripped, so both run stock now (`bgmtl.off.com.apple.springboard`, `bgmtl.off.com.apple.MercuryPoster`). The new iconservicesagent registered on chl.
    * `kern.sweet_gpu` now reads `recoveries=2 hangs=2`. One more hang this boot and the GPU is lost until a reboot. So no more GPU experiments on boot 51.
    * Read the counters with `/var/root/sweetgpu` (source `gpu/kernel-test/sweetgpu.c`). The kernel log ring is only readable from recovery.
  * **Both hangs were SMMU translation faults, not stuck shaders.** Source: the Bergamota app's live USB log, `logs/usb-20261026-162513.txt`, `RECOVERY 1/3` at line 724741 and `RECOVERY 2/3` at line 759858. Kernel-assigned GPU addresses (chl buffers and IOSurface imports alike) all sit at 0x80000000-0x86000000 (measured with `/var/root/chl-test/iova`, no GPU work).
    * Hang 1: FAR 0x17edce200, far outside every allocation, so a garbage pointer. It came right after SpringBoard built `fa16kfmha4`, which fits the early-return miscompile (fixed).
    * Hang 2: FAR `0?cce69d80` (one character lost on USB); it may be a freed BO. It came while SpringBoard on chl was building its icon pipelines (`plane_image_stretch`, `primitive_image`, blur) right after I killed the Gallium iconservicesagent and PosterBoard; `submitted 26707 completed 26698`, IB1 0x80310000. Gallium-era jetsam kills of iconservicesagent (6 on boot 43) never faulted, so suspect chl first.
    * Before guessing further: give the kernel's fault report the owner of FAR and IB1 (pid of the connection whose GEM covers it, or "freed N ms ago by pid P"). The kernel has a client pid list (0186) but no owner per iova.
  * **Boots 52-53 (Wi-Fi reboots, 2026-10-26 ~15:22-15:45 phone time).**
    * Boot 52: backboardd started on chl and was healthy. After SpringBoard joined it, an SMMU fault came (FAR 0ccc41080). backboardd's dead-man wrote `bgmtl-bb.off`, so backboardd is stock again.
    * Boot 53, with SpringBoard on chl and `TU_DEBUG=sysmem`: another fault (FAR 0cc67b000, page-aligned), so GMEM tiling is ruled out. All four SpringBoard faults came as it started drawing icon images (`primitive_image`, `plane_image_stretch`), and every FAR sits around 0xcc... inside the kernel's GVM (0x80000000-0x100000000).
    * Suspect: client-memory ("wrapped") IOSurfaces. They are mapped read-only with no tail page, and the GPU reads past their last row into the guard page after every BO. Chlorophyll 9dd07a9 copies them instead of mapping them. **Not tested yet.**
    * Then my own bug: the first shared-layout build double-freed layouts in `-[BGMTLRenderPipeline dealloc]`. SpringBoard crashed, and launchd logged "rebooting due to critical process crashes: SpringBoard" (my 4 kill -9 restarts counted as well). The phone rebooted twice in a crash loop until I wrote the SpringBoard kill switch right after SSH came up. Fixed in 9dd07a9; chl_test 31/31 covers it.
    * Also in 9dd07a9: the Turnip shader disk cache is on (it was disabled in the build), and `/private/var/tmp/chl.tudebug` passes Turnip's `TU_DEBUG` to every chl process (it is empty now).
    * **State now:** backboardd, SpringBoard and iconservicesagent run stock (kill switches `bgmtl-bb.off`, `bgmtl.off.com.apple.springboard`, `bgmtl.off.com.apple.iconservicesagent`; PosterBoard's tripped too). Mercury and the Photos poster run on chl. The fixed build is installed.
    * **Next, on a fresh boot:** clear the SpringBoard kill switch and restart it once. Watch for `copied, not imported` lines (are icon surfaces wrapped?) and for SMMU faults in the Bergamota USB log. Only then clear `bgmtl-bb.off`.
  * PosterBoard's old kill switch (Gallium era) was moved to `/var/root/bgmtl.off.PosterBoard.gallium`.
  * New diagnostic: `/private/var/tmp/bgmtl.refuse` (fragment-name prefixes; it is empty now) makes bgmtl dump and refuse those pipelines; `bgmtl.refuse.build` builds and times them without drawing.
  * **Next, at the next boot (user OK needed):** first clear `bgmtl.off.com.apple.springboard` and `bgmtl.off.com.apple.MercuryPoster` so everything starts on chl. Then watch `sweetgpu` (hangs) and `bgmtl.log` (failures, DEAD-MAN). If hangs come back, find out which process they follow before anything else.
* **LATEST (2026-10-26 late): the Metal device (`src/bgmtl.m`) runs on chl, and every Metal probe passes on the phone.** Chlorophyll `turnip` 6a27a3b; details in `docs/TURNIP.md` section 3b, "Metal on chl".
  * `bgmtl.m` is rewritten onto chl. Encoders record straight into chl command buffers; there is no BGWork and no bg_lock. The Gallium version is in git history only.
  * Built by Chlorophyll `turnip/build-metal.sh`. It needs `GPU_METAL_WORK=~/git/iAndroid/work-chlorophyll` and `BERGAMOTA_GPU=<a Bergamota checkout>/gpu`; without `BERGAMOTA_GPU`, pacstrip silently does not run and the binaries die with SIGILL on the phone. Test copies are in `/var/root/metal-turnip`.
  * Phone results:
    * `bgmtl_probe` 12/12, `bgmtl_render_probe` 18/18.
    * `bgmtl_layer_probe` 3664/3664 pixels.
    * `bgmtl_xr2p_test`, `bgmtl_texpool_test` and `bgmtl_adddev` pass.
    * CARenderer probe: scenes 0, 3, 4 and 7 match the CPU renderer (diff 0.12-0.22; Gallium gets 0.24 on scene 3 and 0.40 on scene 4). Scenes 5 and 6 blur correctly. Scenes 1 and 2 show the same vertical flip against the CPU reference as on Gallium.
  * **Fixed: framebuffer fetch on Turnip.** ir3 serves the frontend's `load_output(fb_fetch_output)` only through a driver "fb read" descriptor, which Turnip lacks. So every `[[color(n)]]` read returned nothing: rounded clips drew as octagons, masks were off, backdrop materials went unblurred. The bridge now emits input-attachment loads. chl enables `VK_KHR_dynamic_rendering_local_read` and `VK_EXT_rasterization_order_attachment_access`. QuartzCore has 16 such shaders.
  * Also fixed:
    * frontend `param_glsl_type` for i8/i1 parameters;
    * exact `ffma` becomes `fmad`;
    * the command buffer is retired before it is marked done.
  * Known, older than chl: the probes hang at exit, spinning a CPU, until `timeout` kills them (Gallium does the same).
  * **Not done yet:**
    * backboardd on chl. This needs the user's OK: it is a system install of libchlorophyll + inject, replacing libbgair.
    * Per-draw dirty tracking in chl.
    * Delete Gallium from the tree (T6).
  * Earlier the same day: Mesa 26.2.5 + chl, 29/29 `chl_test` checks (`/var/root/chl-test`, IOSurface import included); 5.0-7.5 us/draw through the Metal-shaped encoder (Gallium 9-17, production 100-200). Tree `work-chlorophyll/mesa-turnip26`; host checker `air_vk_check` in `mesa-turnip26/build-host` (now also reads bare `.bc` dumps from `BGMTL_DUMP`).
* Stand-in: `drmSyncobjQuery2` stub (Bergamota 147403f).
* **User: "focus on converting the driver to run on turnip", then "drop Gallium completely and replace it with Turnip".** Full write-up: Chlorophyll `docs/TURNIP.md` (research, build, phone results, Turnip-only port plan T1-T7).
* **Done:**
  * Mesa 25.0.7 Turnip built for iOS arm64e in a separate, Gallium-free tree `work-chlorophyll/mesa-turnip`, by Chlorophyll `turnip/build-turnip.sh` with `patches/turnip-ios.patch`. `mesa-air/build-ios` (the installed libbgair) is untouched.
  * The stand-in `gpu/freedreno-sweet/sweet_drm.c` gained binary syncobjs emulated on the kernel's fences, plus `drmGetDevices2`. These are additions only: Gallium still sees no syncobj cap and msm 1.4.
  * `tu_smoke` on the phone (from `/var/root/turnip-test`, nothing installed system-wide): `Turnip Adreno (TM) 630, Vulkan 1.3.305`; clear + triangle + readback **PASS** (8192/8192 triangle pixels).
  * **Per-draw CPU, same 200-draw frame: Turnip 3.4-4.2 us/draw (0.7-0.8 ms per frame), Gallium `rwa_render_draw` 9-17 us/draw.** Production backboardd is at 100-200 us/draw, mostly above the driver (BGWork objects, per-setBytes GEM buffers, bg_lock replay). So the runtime must record straight into VkCommandBuffers (plan T3); swapping the driver under the old runtime is not enough.
* **Fixes that were needed:** `CTR_EL0` read at EL0 = SIGILL on our XNU (line size from sysctl); no `pthread_condattr_setclock`; no KMS/display WSI; weak entry points (`b_lundef=false`); IOKit BO maps; kernel-assigned iovas (one shared GPU address space); Homebrew SPIRV-Tools leaking into the cross link (`pkg_config_libdir`). Needs `brew install glslang`.
* **Research:**
  * UniXNU (BSD-2-Clause-Patent; Mesa files MIT) runs Turnip on XNU (A740) with its own `tu_knl_darwin.cc`. It is the template for a later native backend (T7).
  * Snapintosh (A8xx, IOGPU kext, EL2 hypervisor) has nothing reusable. Its `compat/adreno-ib-tables.h` is GPL-2.0: avoid.
* **Next (T1):**
  * `air/air_metal_vk.c` (`rwa_*` on Vulkan, inside one dylib with Turnip and libair).
  * NIR from `air_to_nir` via `vk_shader_module_handle_from_nir`, plus a binding bridge pass (flat Gallium indices to set 0 push descriptors + push constants).
  * Check: `test/bgair_test.c` 14/14 on the A630.
* **Not done:**
  * Nothing merged or pushed. The `gpu/metal` submodule pointer is NOT bumped (807c3a7 is local only).
  * Not installed in any system process.

* **ICON GLYPHS FIXED (boot 46, 17:12Z queued from ABL fastboot, iOS up ~17:13Z, user OK; SpringBoard restarted 10:19 PDT with inject 3614cf0e):** every home-screen and dock icon shows its glyph (FaceTime, Calendar, Photos, Mail, Notes, Reminders, Clock, News, TV, Games, App Store, Maps, Health, Wallet, Settings, Phone, Safari, Messages, Music). **How it works on iOS 26:** SpringBoard's RenderBox draws the glyphs into shared RGB10A8_2P_XR10 atlases (448x448 and 448x320 IOSurfaces, one 130x130 cell per icon: load action Load + scissor), backboardd draws each icon as a 128x128 quad cut from those atlases (`VfxU10Xh+TimgA2Xhfn_Isrc`, texture 3). **Cause:** a new atlas reports IOSurface seed 0 until its first write unlock; `rwaTexture` used 0 as "never decoded" (`_surfSeed = seed ? seed : 1`), so until Chlorophyll's first write-back every use re-decoded the empty atlas over the texture (37x for atlas 631 in the trace), wiping each icon drawn but not yet written back; which survived depended on timing (a different handful every run). **Fix** gpu/metal 595a91b: decoded-once is its own flag (`_surfFresh`); test `bgmtl_xr2p_test.m` covers it (fails on the old code). Found with a backboardd trace (`bgmtl.tracing`, 10.7 MB in `scratchpad/tr6`, battery 5 % at the time) + `scratchpad/drawmap.py` (draws per icon cell) + the xr10 log. Switches `bgmtl.xr10log` and `bgmtl.tracing` removed afterwards. **Still open:** plain-blue wallpaper after a SpringBoard restart (fine after boot); widget "Waiting..."; lock-screen clock missing; iOS 26 does not charge (sweetcharger=2).
* **WALLPAPER AFTER A SPRINGBOARD RESTART FIXED (boot 46, 10:17-10:44 PDT, inject 38e3a280):** the plain blue is the poster's placeholder: backboardd draws a full-screen fill rgb(0.082, 0.277, 0.467) under everything; at boot Mercury's drawable (828x1792 BGRA IOSurface, e.g. 639) is drawn over it, after a respring no Mercury surface is sampled at all although MercuryPosterExtension renders (18 frames traced). Relaunching Mercury did not help (lock screen blue too); **a fresh PosterBoard always shows the wallpaper, a PosterBoard that outlived the previous SpringBoard never does** (tested on and off Chlorophyll). Fix (gpu/metal): the inject in SpringBoard SIGTERMs a PosterBoard whose start time predates SpringBoard's (`restart_stale_posterboard`, off with `/private/var/tmp/bgmtl.nopbrestart`, log line `PosterBoard N predates this SpringBoard`); SpringBoard launches a fresh one. **PosterBoard is on Chlorophyll** (user's call): `/Applications/PosterBoard.app/PosterBoard` re-signed with weak `LC_LOAD_DYLIB /usr/local/bgi.dylib` + `SweetAdrenoUserClient` (same identifier `PosterBoard-5555...`, 56 entitlement keys), bundle ID in the allow list; it registers and has submitted no GPU work. Backups on the phone: `/var/root/PosterBoard.pbhook` (the binary just replaced, with the root image's `pbhook.dylib` load) and `/var/root/PosterBoard.orig` (older, 2026-10-25, without pbhook). Root-image scripts do not carry this yet (nor SpringBoard's). Unified log on this setup: logd does not persist messages after boot to Persist/Special (only 17:13Z in the files), so `scratchpad/ios3.logarchive` could not show the PosterBoard difference.

Remaining per frame on backboardd's display thread (synchronous commits, `bgmtl-bb.sync`): CA's own work (QuartzCore ~40 % of samples), encode ~18 ms / 160 draws (rwa_render_draw -> fd6_draw_vbos/emit_3d_state, BO allocs), GPU wait ~7 ms. **GPU sits at level 1 = 257 MHz of 710 (governor `gov`)** per sweetmetrics -- check whether it ramps during animations (my poll loop wrote nothing; redo). ### >>> iOS 18 on beryllium, 2026-10-24: lock screen, frame order, Linux builds, Metal CoreAnimation harness <<<
Phone state: boot `enxerto-boot-combined9.img` (xnu 0227 on branch combined), userdata = `ios18-root-full.simg` of 2026-10-24 02:07 plus live edits below. Remote testing: `sweetshot` (kern.sweet_fb_shot, xnu 0224), `sweettouch` (tap/swipe/home/lock), `sweetlog SECONDS WORD...` (unified log over SSH: OSLogStore over a .logarchive of links to /var/db/diagnostics + uuidtext; phone copy in /private/var/tmp/sweetlog, not yet in a root build).
* **Lock screen replayed its wake sequence forever** (wallpaper zoom, lock glyph, "Swipe up to open", never slept): without CommCenter, SpringBoard's SBTelephonyManager looked up `com.apple.commcenter.xpc` about once a second, failed, and refreshed the idle timer. Fix: CommCenter is back as its non-cellular job (`com.apple.CommCenter-noncellular.plist` without `_LimitLoadFromDeviceTree`; idles at 0 %), vmd removed (Visual Voicemail spun at 80 % of a core). root_tweaks18 `launchd()`; on the phone the edited launchd.plist is installed (`launchd.plist.pre-commcenter` kept). Side effect: CommCenter sets the real date/time.
* **Stale frame on every wake** (xnu 0227): CoreAnimation's first swap after display-on has no layer; the copy path scanned its back buffer as it was (an old frame). Now it is cleared to black.
* **Lock screen animation frames out of order** (metal 501a3c4): the ES compositor (gpu/eagl/src/eagl.m) re-uploads a copied IOSurface only when `IOSurfaceGetSeed` changes, and GPU writes by BGMTL never moved the seed, so Mercury's three drawables showed frames one cycle old. BGMTL now does a write lock/unlock on every IOSurface it rendered into after the fence. Installed on the phone in /usr/local/lib/bgmtl/bgmtl-inject.dylib (fae989b8; old one kept as `.pre-seed`); a root rebuild needs the new dylib in the bgmtl dir.
* **Frame pacing (2026-10-24):** CoreAnimation's IOMFBFlipBook keeps one swap in flight and recycles the previous surface once a swap is reported done, so scanning its surfaces in place either waits for the latch every frame (`sweetfb_swapdepth=1`, 41 ms median, ~25 fps) or tears at the bottom (`swapdepth=2`, ~32 fps; `CA_FORCE_TRIPLE_BUFFERING` only affects layer backing stores). Patch 0235 `sweetfb_scancopy=1` copies each frame (~6.5 ms, work loop) into three framebuffer-owned low-pool buffers and completes the swap after the copy: 29-33 fps during home swipes, no tearing (user-checked). Image `ci-0235-scancopy.img` = CI kernel + `beryllium-hi-3x-gpufwz.adt` (the plain ADT has no GPU firmware) + `sweetfb_swapdepth=2 sweetfb_scancopy=1`. Dirty regions and async commits still break drawing (A/B 2026-10-24). Locally built kernels (Xcode 27 clang 21) boot without the GPU: see 0236.
* **Blur transparent once after a boot** (backboardd logged `GL_INVALID_OPERATION` per frame): not reproducible on the next boot; `kill -9` of backboardd fixes it. Not the clock jump (replayed) nor dark mode. Watch for it.
* **Linux `renegade root`** (friend's PC): berga-tools-v2 (prerelease) ships dropbear/dash/toybox (+licenses) and sweetshot/sweettouch, `ssh_out()` falls back to the cache; dropbear `-R` when the host has no dropbearkey; verify skips the intentional BlueTool stub; seal sets the HFSX folder count Linux's hfsplus leaves unset on the root folder (fsck rc 8 otherwise). Verified end to end except a full Linux run.
* **Metal compositor, step 1 (offscreen harness, branch metal 9ae2439):** `bgmtl_carenderer_probe` now gets CoreAnimation's Metal renderer drawing on BGMTLDevice: fat metallibs load their AIR slice, binary archives open empty, `commandBufferWithDescriptor:`, command-buffer SPI no-ops, buffer-offset setters, a logged zero/no-op fallback on every BGMTL class, `BGMTL_TRACE=1`. Gap list for step 2: (1) vertex fetch wrong (scene 0: triangles fan wrongly, colours wrong: suspect vertex descriptor formats/strides or the index-buffer offset); (2) image/mask fragment shaders store `[[depth]]` with no depth attachment and are refused, then CoreAnimation aborts (drop the store in libbgair); (3) `newBufferWithBytesNoCopy:length:options:deallocator:` missing (text); (4) scene 0 sometimes reads back all zeros (sync/readback race). Blur pipelines (`downsample_4`, `narrow_blur_11`) compile. No GPU hang in any run.

### >>> Branch `gpu-kernel` (2026-10-23): A630 keep-alive + msm-style IOKit client, built, NOT run on the phone <<<
`docs/GPU_KERNEL.md`: patches 0180-0182 (xnu-11215 series): boot-arg `sweetgpu=8` = stage 7 + CP fill self-test + GPU kept up (GMU keep-alive, OOB_GPU held, zap left running) + IOService `SweetAdreno630` whose user client exposes the Linux msm ioctls (GET_PARAM, GEM_NEW/INFO/CPU_PREP/FINI, SUBMIT, WAIT_FENCE, SUBMITQUEUE_NEW/CLOSE, GEM_CLOSE + our SURFACE_BO/BO_SYNC/INFO) as selectors for the Mesa winsys (`xnu/gpu-abi/sweet_adreno_abi.h`). ### >>> Bluetooth on iOS 18 (branch `bt18`, 2026-10-23): findings, root/ADT fixes, kernel 0136 from xnu11215 needed <<<
* iOS 18.7.8 bluetoothd/BlueTool use the same transport model as 16.7 (checked in IDA): `BT_HCI_TRANSPORT=TRANSPORT:DRIVER:DEVICE` (H4BC:POSIX:com.apple.uart.bluetooth,3000000), kernel control socket, BlueTool XPC `com.apple.BlueTool` (kMsgId RunBuiltinScript/RunCommand, kMsgArgs script boot|init|deepsleep). New in 18: "Booting chipset simple and fast" (start 0x100376164), but only for PCIe transports 7/8/9; for H4BC it returns 11 and falls back to the BlueTool `boot` script, so the stub still applies.
* **BT_DEVICE_ADDRESS blocker (the iOS 16 pause) is by design in 18 too:** `start` (0x1000bb5d0) sscanf()s the env var and then executes `STR WZR,[dword_100A3F280]` / `STRH WZR,[word_100A3F284]` (0x1000bb844/848), so the env address is always discarded. The only usable source is registry entry `marconi-bt` or `bluetooth` with `local-mac-address` (IORegistryEntrySearchCFProperty, plane IODeviceTree), then `/dev/cu.bluetooth`, `/dev/uart.bluetooth` or that registry entry must exist. Phone before the fix: "Unable to match bluetooth service in device tree / Not a valid MAC address (all 00s) / No bluetooth on this device", exit 0 (KeepAlive.SuccessfulExit=false: no restart).
* **Fix 1 (ADT, no kernel code):** the rebrand dropped the `/arm-io/bluetooth` node from develop's tools/mkadt.py (it exists on main). Restored (`bluetooth_node`, BT_MAC, board beryllium only). SweetPlatformExpert (0046) already publishes every /arm-io child except gic, so the nub appears. ADT: `shim/out/beryllium-hi-3x-bt18.adt` (= beryllium-hi-3x.adt + bluetooth node + enxerto strings).
* **Fix 2 (root):** `renegade root` (mkroot-full.py stage_tweaks) never called `rt.bluetooth(root)`: bluetoothd had no BT_HCI_TRANSPORT and BlueTool was stock. Now called (launchd.plist env, BlueTool stub as /usr/sbin/BlueTool with BlueTool.orig, /usr/local/bin/hcitest); the stub path in root_tweaks18.py was also wrong (`../research` -> `../../research`). Needs a root reseal/flash or the launchd cache edit; research/sweetnet/{bluetool-stub,hcitest} are plain arm64 and ran on iOS 18 as-is.
* Kernel half: patch 0136 (bt-hci-kctl, `com.apple.uart.bluetooth` control + sweetbt=1) is on origin/xnu11215 (7d9e2c9); boot with `sweetwlan=2 sweetbt=1`. Not booted on 18 yet.
* Next after boot: `hcitest` (kernel ctl alone), then bluetoothd. Known further iOS 18 issue seen with a bluetoothd copy whose address/registry checks were patched out (bytes 0x1000bb844, 0x1000bb848 -> NOP, 0x1000bba9c CBZ -> B 0x1000bbb84) run as mobile: it aborts "LeSecurityManager hit a keychain problem" (0xbd15dead, main thread blocked in mach_msg to securityd) - keychain/securityd availability for bluetoothd is the likely second blocker, not yet analysed (unified log not decoded: phone went offline).

`docs/IOS18_PORT.md`: pacstrip rewriter (`tools/renegade/`), iOS 18 restore-ramdisk bundle (real dyld + flat libSystem dylibs, no cache), kernel patch 0131 (`sweetarm64e=1`: arm64e exec grading, AMFI dyld policy 0x5a, `ptrauth_disabled=1`), `tools/renegade/run-poc.sh`. Unverified on hardware; see the doc for how to read failures and the stage 2 plan (rewritten cache + slide v5).

### >>> iOS 18 full root, boot `logs/usb-20261020-224536.txt` (2026-10-21 analysis, nothing run, image untouched) <<<
Symbolication needs no raw image: `tools/renegade/hfsx.py SIMG ls|cat|get|dsc` reads the 15 GB simg in place; `tools/renegade/dsctools/` (docstring of symb.py) symbolicates/disassembles the PAC-stripped cache (ObjC method names, objc_msgSend[sel] stubs, direct stubs, os_log format strings per image).
* **backboardd** (16x, identical): `+[NSURL fileURLWithPath:nil]` <- BaseBoard `BSSystemSharedResourcesDirectoryForIdentifier(nil)` <- `BSSystemSharedDirectoryForIdentifier(nil)` (BaseBoard+0x7c300; for nil it falls back to `[[NSBundle mainBundle] bundleIdentifier]` only to log) <- `container_system_path_for_identifier(NULL, &err)` returned NULL. That is libsystem_containermanager class 12 (Data/System, the table order of `containers/Data/System`), identifier = the caller's own signing id (com.apple.backboardd, preserved by the re-sign), asked from containermanagerd over XPC (client side is trivial: query_create(12,..)+object_get_path). The nil path is `<system container of com.apple.backboardd>` (+ `/Library` + caches for the shader cache, backboardd+0x385cc). Same failure on iOS 16 (NOTES 2026-10-17 `backboardd EXCEPTION FOUND`), there fixed by /private/var/containers + patches 0050/0052.
* **It is one global failure: containermanagerd (MCM) answers no container lookup.** lsd (19+) aborts in `container_query_get_single_result` (class 10 InternalDaemon, persona CURRENT, flags 0x900000001, then logs `container_query_get_last_error` and `abort`); mobileactivationd's `main` line 281 error text is "Failed to copy container path(s)." (brk in the NSError helper); passd dies in `FinanceKit.CoreDataProvider.shared()` (try!, the store comes from financed over `com.apple.financed.service.coredatastore`; financed itself crashes in boot 2); appprotectiond in `AppProtection ManagementExpert` (swift_unexpectedError, CoreData), replicatord in `ReplicatorCore SQLDatabase` ("Unable to open database store ...; all attempts to recover failed"); spaceattributiond/fairplayd.H2/PerfPowerServices/webinspectord relaunch ~175x each WITHOUT a crash (xpcproxy 1022 spawns in boot 1). findmydeviced: NSException from Foundation (Locale/plist path code), not looked at further. cryptexd is unrelated: trap inside libimage4 `img4_firmware_new_from_fd` (no Image4 environment); harmless to remove.
* **Why MCM refuses is NOT yet known** (os_log never reaches the console). Ruled out by inspection: entitlements of backboardd/lsd/containermanagerd/usermanagerd/launchd (container/persona-mgmt ones present, DER+XML intact), signing identifiers, users/groups (/etc/master.passwd has root, mobile, _installd; group systemusers), csops ops 0-17 all implemented, sandbox ops 15/16 are the same as in the working iOS 16 boot, persona syscalls compiled in and entitled, dirs /private/var/containers/{Bundle,Data/System,Shared/SystemGroup,Temp} exist. New in iOS 18 vs the healthy iOS 16 log (`logs/boot2/unified.jsonl`: "Personas are supported (static): YES", "Got userPersonaAttributes on try 0 ... Persona ID:1000", "Found personal persona"): (1) MCM is split into agent (containermanagerd, system/bundle classes in `proxy` mode, `--kernel-upcall=yes`) and containermanagerd_system (`exec containermanagerd_system` is new, started once); (2) MCM gets user identities from usermanagerd (`-[UMUserManager personaGenerationIdentifierWithError:]`, entitlements `com.apple.usermanagerd.persona.fetch`), and iOS 18 usermanagerd allocates kernel personas WITH a path (`kpersona_palloc`, "/private/var/PersonaVolumes") and AKSIdentityAddPersona; the iOS 18 `UMD_INIT` boot task prints none of the iOS 16 `UMD_INIT: LOADING USER SESSIONS ...` lines, but those are now os_log calls gated by a log-level check (usermanagerd+0x50f80), so that is NOT evidence; usermanagerd never calls AKS in either version; (3) AKS selectors still unsupported (0xe00002c7): keybagd sel 107, securityd sel 10 (wrap key, in 2/32 out 1/40), profiled sel 31. Ranked suspects: usermanagerd persona/session setup (MCM then has no personal persona -> every lookup fails), agent->system proxy, AKS 10/107.
* **Settle it with patch `xnu/patches/0144-sweet-syserr-trace.patch`** (compile-checked on bt138, not built): boot-arg `sweetsyserr` (default list = the daemons above, `*` all, `-` off) prints once per distinct (process, syscall, errno, path) every failing BSD syscall as `SWEET ERR: containermanager[41] mkdir(136) e=45 "/private/var/..."`, incl. persona(494) op/flags, csops, fcntl cmd, __mac_syscall policy/op, proc_info. p_comm is 16 chars (containermanagerd_system = "containermanager"). It cannot see Mach/XPC failures; if nothing relevant shows up the failure is IPC (usermanagerd/launchd lookups) and the next probe is a tiny arm64 tool run by a launchd job that calls `container_query_*` / `UMUserManager` and prints the error codes.
* **SSH**: dropbear IS running (job `org.sweet.dropbear` in launchd.plist, `/usr/local/bin/dropbear` iOS 16 arm64, host keys + authorized_keys present, `-p 192.168.7.2:22`): the log has `[55] Not backgrounding` (listening) and per connection `Child connection from 192.168.7.1:53256` followed 70-110 s later by `Early exit: Failed socket address: Invalid argument` (= getpeername on a socket whose TCP connection is already gone: the forked child needed longer than the client waited to reach `svr_session`; DO_REEXEC and DO_HOST_LOOKUP are 0 in this build, so no fexecve/DNS). The same stall hits every iOS 16 arm64 binary: parent took ~4.6 min from exec to listen, `toybox uname` output ~770 log lines after its exec. Candidates: startup/fork cost of non-arm64e processes on the arm64e cache under load (1022 xpcproxy spawns + ReportCrash for every crash: 448 ReportCrash AKS lines, "Too many corpses"), or a libinfo/launchd IPC wait. `SWEET_QUIET=1 tools/renegade/mkroot-full.sh` (root_tweaks18.quiet) removes cryptexd and throttles the crash/relaunch loopers to 60 s; 0144 with `sweetsyserr=dropbear,toybox` shows what dropbear waits on.
* HFS: boot 2 (same log, line 5313+) mounts the volume dirty (unjournaled root, boot 1 was never unmounted) and then spews `BlockMarkFreeInternal() trying to free unallocated blocks` / `hfs_mark_inconsistent`: re-flash (or fsck from recovery) before every test boot, otherwise the second boot's file operations are unreliable.

**2026-10-21 (later) - first 0144 boot, `logs/usb-20261021-185247.txt` (clean re-flash):** unnamed syscalls decoded (syscalls.master): 38 `crossarch_trap` ENOTSUP (Rosetta probe), 98 `connect` ENOENT (AF_UNIX path missing), 202 `sysctl` ENOENT (sysctlbyname name2oid miss), 266/268 `shm_open`/`sem_open` ENOENT, 286 `gettid` ESRCH, 488 `renameatx_np` ENOENT (RENAME_SWAP onto a not-yet-existing file; Foundation falls back, no rename() error follows); `csops(169|170) op=16 e=34` = CS_OPS_DER_ENTITLEMENTS_BLOB size probe, ERANGE is the protocol (docs/CSOPS.md), the 170 lines in 0144 print the pid, not the op. All benign. usermanagerd's `usersession.kb`/`persona.kb` ENOENT is a fresh data volume (it then tries to write `.writing` files; its unlink of them ENOENT is normal). Containermanagerd + containermanagerd_system are healthy for file work: first-boot class resync, 6 well-known app containers staged and moved into /private/var/mobile/Containers/Data/Application (so MCM HAS a user identity), only EEXIST/ENOENT noise, but NO syscall of MCM ever touches Data/System, Shared/SystemGroup or Data/InternalDaemon for a client request: client requests are rejected before any file operation (identity/persona/authorization), not by the filesystem. Patch `0145-sweet-syserr-trace-decode-mach.patch` (on top of 0144, compile-checked) adds: persona id/uid in every line (`[pid/p1000 u501]`), sysctl names, AF_UNIX connect paths, renameatx args, right csops op, failed Mach traps (`MACH trap N kr=0x..`, invalid trap numbers as 1000+N), launchd/xpcproxy in the default list, and `SWEET EXEC: name[pid] XPC_FLAGS=0x...` at exec. Why: MCM's `Personas are supported (static)` is a flag computed from 5 negatives (no UMUserManager class, os_variant_uses_ephemeral_storage, MGGetBoolAnswer("3kmXfug8VcxLI5yEmsqQKw") diagnostics, xpc_is_system_session() = XPC_FLAGS bit 0x4000 via libxpc init, host_get_multiuser_config_flags < 0); iOS 16 printed YES with an empty reason list; with the flag false MCM falls back to non-persona identities. Also: MCM's log replication writes `<home>/Library/Logs/MobileContainerManager/*.log` (it stat()ed both /private/var/{mobile,root}/Library/Logs/MobileContainerManager) - pull those files from the phone (OrangeFox) for MCM's own reason.

**2026-10-21 (even later) - ROOT CAUSE of the container refusals (logarchive pull `research/ios18-pull-1021-1927`, boot 190147):** the unified log of the failing boot is readable where launchd/xpc/UserManagement log (`unifiedlog_iterator`, ~3 min, 4 M rows: filter by pid while streaming to /dev/stdout, never to a file: 3.3 GB). (1) containermanagerd[71] (agent) asks usermanagerd for the persona list within 30 ms of start: `com.apple.xpc connection ... activating` for `com.apple.mobile.usermanagerd.xpc` -> `Interruption handler invoked` -> `Failed to fetch persona list ... NSCocoaErrorDomain Code=4097 connection to service named ...`; launchd logs `failed activation: name = com.apple.mobile.usermanagerd.xpc ... requestor = usermanagerd[97], error = 37: Operation already in progress` (launchd+0x21e44 launch_service: state spawning -> EINPROGRESS "launch already in progress"; pid != 0 but endpoint not checked in -> EALREADY 37). usermanagerd (RunAtLoad, pid 97) is only being spawned at that moment: lsd/securityd/launchd's own persona lookups fail the same way in the first second. MCM retries ~35 times until 776.71, then serves with no personas and logs no request at all. (2) backboardd's `BSSystemSharedDirectoryForIdentifier` error is `52` = container error table[52] = **XPC_INTERRUPTED_REPLY** (table at libsystem_containermanager `container_get_error_description`: 50 XPC_UNKNOWN_REPLY, 51 XPC_INVALID_REPLY, 52 XPC_INTERRUPTED_REPLY, 53 INVALID_REPLY, 55 NOT_ENTITLED...), i.e. the XPC call to MCM was interrupted, not refused by entitlement/persona logic (the earlier 0x37/0x35 guesses were wrong). Personas themselves exist (p199 system, p200 personal u501; p4294967295 = none yet) - no persona problem, no HFS/EPERM problem (unlink EPERM on Dead/temp.* = unlink() of a directory, removefile falls back to rmdir). mach_msg2_trap(47) kr 0x10004002 = MACH_RCV_INVALID_NAME, one-off per process, benign so far. **Fix (kernel, not booted):** `0147-sweet-exec-delay-mcm.patch` delays the first exec of containermanagerd by 6 s (boot-arg `sweetexecdelay=name:ms,...`, `-` off) so usermanagerd is checked in before MCM's persona fetch; `0146` moves the XPC_FLAGS exec trace after exec_extract_strings (0145's hook ran after ip_strings was consumed: 0 lines). If usermanagerd turns out never to check in (its own self-activation error 37), the next step is a launchd-visible check: pid 97 logs nothing to logd (its os_log is gated), so use 0145's per-process error lines and the logarchive `Simpledump` rows of pid 1.
**2026-10-21 (0147 boot, unified log boot 06B2F3E8):** CORRECTION: launchd's `failed activation ... error = 37` for usermanagerd is NOT the cause by itself: the same line (EPERM, error 1) appears for apsd/timed/nsurlsessiond idswake endpoints, it is a failed self-activation of a non-launching endpoint. What the 0147 log shows: MCM main starts at +43.16, its persona fetch at 43.710 fails 17x within 20 ms (`Interruption handler invoked`, 4097) although the 6 s delay worked (pid 71 xpcproxy at 38.83); launchd logs NO failed lookup for com.apple.mobile.usermanagerd.xpc, so the lookup succeeds and the connection is interrupted: nobody owns the endpoint. usermanagerd (pids 12 and 97) has ZERO unified-log rows in every boot while every other daemon logs. Clients (lsd) run with kernel persona **200 = type SYSTEM_PROXY** (`container ... ['200','SYSTEM_PROXY','9A58E8C7-...']`), i.e. the personal persona of user 501 does not exist: it is created by the usermanagerd daemon at user login, so launchd falls back to the proxy persona; lsd's own failure: `container_query_get_single_result 2->(52) XPC_INTERRUPTED_REPLY`. Conclusion: the usermanagerd DAEMON never gets going (silent after `open nextsession.kb` ENOENT); delaying MCM cannot help. Next (no kernel build): boot with `sweetsample=usermanagerd,containermanager,lsd` (bsd/dev/sweet_sample.c prints pc/lr/bt of every thread after 90 s, then every 120 s; comm names are 16 chars) and symbolicate with tools/renegade/dsctools/crash.py-style lookups (SMP line gives slide and mach header) to see where usermanagerd is blocked.
**2026-10-22 sampler boot (`logs/usb-20261022-201239.txt`, slide 0x156e0000):** usermanagerd[97] has 2 threads in all 7 samples (t0 main: `mach_msg2_trap <- mach_msg <- CFRunLoopRun <- main+0x51478` = idle in the main runloop AFTER "USERMANAGERD SESSION SETUP DONE" (main+0x51430); t1 `__workq_kernreturn` idle). containermanagerd[71] and [100]: identical idle main runloop (`_containermanagerd_main+0xf48 -> CFRunLoopRun`) + idle workq; only the first sample caught an MCM worker thread, doing `MCMCommandOperationReclaimDiskSpace -> forEachAccessibleUserIdentity -> MCMLibraryRepairForUser` (so MCM HAS a user identity and works). Nobody is blocked on keybagd/AKS/semaphores: both are idle-but-unreachable (endpoints not delivering). Next probe: `0148-sweet-sample-ports.patch` (compile-checked) adds `SMP name PORTS recv N psets P queued Q: 0xname:count ...` per sampled process = receive rights and which hold queued messages (queued clients + idle receiver = source never attached / endpoint never checked in).
**2026-10-22 0148 ports:** usermanagerd recv 18 psets 0 queued 0 (no client message pending; MCM[71] one right 0xe03 with 1 queued). 18 receive rights without names cannot tell whether `com.apple.mobile.usermanagerd.xpc` is one of them: `0149-sweet-sample-ports-detail.patch` prints one line per receive right `SMP name R 0xname msgs/seqno srights/mscount/sorights knote/nsreq/kotype` (seqno = messages ever received; knote = a dispatch/kevent source attached). Reading: service ports have srights>=1 (launchd's send) and mscount>=1; a service port with seqno 0 never got a client message; with knote 0 nobody would dequeue.
### 2026-10-22 0149 port dump reading, 0150 probe

- usermanagerd completed its setup (SESSION SETUP DONE, then CFRunLoopRun); listeners with knote=1 are attached. Only 0xe0b (seqno 23), 0xa03 (12), 0x1003 (4) ever received anything; 0x190f (6 send rights, knote 1) and the other listeners stay at seqno 0, unchanged over 120 s. No bail-out before listener creation; usermanagerd is idle and is not being sent the .xpc requests.
- containermanagerd ports are busy (0x1603 seqno 623->1001): clients reach MCM, which may drop the usermanagerd-dependent request (client sees error 52, interrupted).
- 0150-sweet-sample-send-rights.patch (compile-checked, apply after 0149) dumps each sampled task's send rights as `SMP name S 0xname -> pid P 0xrecvname msgs/seqno knote`. Compare lsd/MCM rights pointing at usermanagerd vs launchd (pid 1) to see whether the .xpc endpoint handed to clients is 0x190f or a launchd-owned port.

### 2026-10-22 0150 result, 0151 launchd probe

- 0150: MCM reaches usermanagerd (pid97 0x110b seqno 27). backboardd holds send rights to three launchd-owned ports (0x3e03, 0x16303 with 6 queued each, 0x41517 with 1), seqno 0, knote 0. launchd's receive rights of a job that is not running (crash-loop/throttle: lsd) are not watched, so requests queue; knote 0 can also mean the knote sits on a port set (ip_in_pset) rather than the port.
- 0151-sweet-sample-launchd-ports.patch (compile-checked, after 0150): per receive right adds `pset`, `sls` (ip_sync_link_state) and the first queued message's id/size; for a sampled `launchd` it lists only ports with queued messages or in a pset. Boot with sweetsample=...,launchd.

### 2026-10-22 0151 result, 0152 launchd kevent/spawn tracer

- 0151: launchd[1] holds ~45 service-port receive rights with 1-6 queued 'w00t' XPC messages each, seqno 0, pset0, knote 0: no demand watch on any of them (5 is the default qlimit, so queues are full and clients block/fail).
- Static look at launchd 22H352: all mach_port_* calls go through one wrapper cluster at 0x10000b6xx-0x10000b8xx (mach_port_construct, assert_attributes flavor 8, set_attributes, request_notification); dispatch_source_create has 6 callers (timer/signal/proc/vnode-like, not obviously MACH_RECV); dispatch_mach_* are imported but no direct bl call site in __text. Watches are therefore probably set up inside libdispatch/libxpc, i.e. kevent_qos/kevent_id EVFILT_MACHPORT.
- 0152-sweet-launchd-kevent-spawn-trace.patch (compile-checked, after 0144): for pid 1 logs every kevent/kevent64/kevent_qos/kevent_id change item with filter EVFILT_MACHPORT ("SWEET KQ launchd ...", up to 400 lines, flags/fflags/qos/ext) and every failing posix_spawn with path and psa_flags ("SWEET SPAWN").

### 2026-10-22 0152 result, 0153 EINVAL-site tracer

- 0152: launchd's EVFILT_MACHPORT registrations succeed (kevent_id on workloop 0x10075b650; ADD flags 0x385 = ADD|ENABLE|DISPATCH|UDATA_SPECIFIC|VANISHED, fflags 0x8000). The same idents later get flags 0x186 = DELETE: e.g. 0x2d03 and 0x3e03 ADD at log line 64/68 and DELETE at 184/188. So the stuck ports (knote 0) were not dropped by our kernel: launchd removed the demand watch itself when it launched the owning job, and the job never checked in (spawn failed or the job died). 0x184 lines (ENABLE|DISPATCH, no ADD) are DISPATCH re-arms.
- Failing posix_spawn: SpringBoard (psa_flags 0x460c = SIGDEF|SIGMASK|NANO_ALLOCATOR|SETSID|CLOEXEC_DEFAULT, attr_size 248) and xpcproxy x3 (0x400c) return EINVAL, so those jobs' services (SpringBoard has dozens) stay unwatched and unserved. 8796 posix_spawn has ~15 EINVAL sites (file/port actions size checks, posix cred ngroups, coalition without resource coalition, coalitions_set_roles, persona adopt), flags themselves are all known.
- 0153-sweet-einval-sites.patch (compile-checked, apply after 0145-0147): every `EINVAL;` return in bsd/kern/kern_exec.c (30 sites) and kern_persona.c (5) prints `SWEET EINVAL file:line pid N`; map the line against the patched file to see the exact check.

### 2026-10-22 0154 boot: containermanagerd crash-loop = sqlite init failure on HFS EINVAL; 0155 probe

- containermanagerd is not exiting quietly: CRASH lines are printed under the truncated name `containermanager` (13 crashes, sig 5, same pc every time = ContainerManagerCommon+0xbd0b0, a brk right after `_os_crash("Failed to initialize database, err = %d, xerr = %d")`; symbolicated with dsctools crash.py + dcache.py on the extracted cache). Each crash is followed by a relaunch, so backboardd's MCM requests hit a MCM that dies during startup -> container_system_path_for_identifier returns NULL -> the NSURL nil path exception.
- Reason it cannot open its DB: the root HFS reports `hfs_mark_inconsistent: Runtime corruption detected on iOSRoot` 64 times during the boot (first one at keybagd `Unable to set /private/var//tmp to class D!`), `BlockMarkFreeInternal() trying to free unallocated blocks <3238859,7>`, and file operations return EINVAL: open/open_dprotected_np of `/private/var/root/Library/MobileContainerManager/System/boot.txt`, stat64 of `/private/var/mobile/Library/MobileContainerManager/references.sqlite3-wal`, unlinkat of run sockets. The image itself is clean: a full catalog scan (tools: scan_cat.py logic, 422441 records) finds no bad modes, no size > blocks.
- Likely EINVAL source: hfs_getnewvnode sanity check (ca_blocks < cf_blocks or size > blocks, or VBAD mode) which also calls hfs_mark_inconsistent; or hfs_catalog.c cnid==0 checks. Not proven.
- 0155-sweet-hfs-inconsistent-sites.patch (compile-checked): a macro in hfs.h makes each hfs_mark_inconsistent call print `SWEET HFSINC file:line pid reason`.

### 2026-10-22 ROOT CAUSE of the "interrupted" XPC replies: mach_port_is_connection_for_service -> KERN_NOT_SUPPORTED (0156)

- Unified log of the clean 0155 boot, shared-cache strings resolved: a shared-cache format-string offset in the log is simply (cache address - 0x180000000), so `rd(0x180000000 + off)` gives the format string (patched research/macos-UnifiedLogs message.rs to print `Unknown shared string message @<offset>`).
- lsd logged 878 x `[%p] Failed to verify check-in connection port was derived from the listener connection port (kr=%d, sandbox_message_filter_id=#%llx)`, containermanagerd/containermanagerd_system `Bogus check-in attempt. Ignoring.`, MCM `Failed to fetch persona generation ... Got error from remote proxy` + ~30 `Re-initialization successful; calling out ... XPC_ERROR_CONNECTION_INTERRUPTED`.
- libxpc (listener side, `_xpc_connection_...` at libxpc+0x13ba4) calls the MIG routine mach_port_is_connection_for_service(task, connection_port, service_port, &filter_id) for every new peer check-in and accepts only KERN_SUCCESS (0) or KERN_INVALID_CAPABILITY (20); anything else = bogus check-in, the peer is dropped.
- xnu-8796 `mach_port_is_connection_for_service` returned KERN_NOT_SUPPORTED when no Sandbox message-filter callbacks are registered (`mach_msg_filter_at_least(VERSION_1)` false; we have no Sandbox kext). So EVERY XPC listener that uses a service port rejected every client: usermanagerd/MCM/lsd/... never answered, clients got interrupted replies, containers and persona generation never resolved, backboardd path nil, lsd crash loop.
- Fix: xnu/patches/0156-ios18-service-port-no-filter.patch (compile-checked on bt138): drop that early return; without a service-port sblabel the function falls through to "nothing to check" = KERN_SUCCESS, filter id 0 (non service ports still give KERN_INVALID_CAPABILITY).

### 2026-10-23 0156 boot: black screen = IOSurface limits struct v2 (0157); ue0 IPv4 loss = no BPF on ue0 (0158)

- Black screen: backboardd logs 6065 x `IOSurface creation failed. IOSurface open failed` and QuartzCore 6068 x `CAIOSurfaceCreate: failed to allocate IOSurface`. (Unified-log strings resolved with cache addr = offset + 0x180000000; for non-cache binaries the offset is the file offset into the main/bundle binary: patched message.rs prints `@offset` for UUIDText failures too.) IOSurface.framework 22H352 `_iosConnectInitalize`: finds IOCoreSurfaceRoot (we have it), IOServiceOpen type 0, then IOConnectCallMethod(selector 13, struct out 0x28) and requires the u16 at +0x22 == 1, else logs `IOSurface.framework versus IOSurface.kext version mismatch`, closes the connection and every later create fails. SweetSurface returned the iOS 16 layout (0x18 bytes, version byte at 0x16). New layout (from _IOSurfaceClientGetPropertyAlignment/Maximum): +0 u32 align-1 (Offset/PlaneOffset/PlaneBase), +4 u32 align-1 (BytesPerRow/PlaneBytesPerRow), +8 u64 max BytesPerRow, +0x10 u64 max Width, +0x18 u64 max Height, +0x20 u8 flag, +0x22 u16 version. 0157 returns it.
- ue0: IPConfiguration (IPConfiguration.bundle, strings decoded from the bundle binary) logged `arp_client_open_fd: bpf_setif(ue0) failed` + `MANUAL ue0: arp probe failed` every 1.5 s, `siocprotodetach(ue0)`, `removing network service`: our legacy ifnet has no BPF tap, so the MANUAL ARP probe never succeeds and the kernel's static 192.168.7.2 is flushed. 0158 does bpf_attach(ue0, DLT_EN10MB).
- dropbear: root_tweaks18.py job now `-p 22` (v4+v6); image must be rebuilt/patched (two plists: job + System/Library/xpc/launchd.plist) to take effect.

### 2026-10-23 SpringBoard SIGSEGV in vImageDeepmap2Decode = IOSurface create-result layout (0159)

- Crash: byte write to base+2 (LA08 surface 312x202: bytesPerElement 2 was read as the surface Offset) beyond the mapping. IOSurface.framework 22H352 copies the create/lookup result to client+0x70 with a new layout (read from the IOSurfaceClientGet* getters): 0x00 base, 0x08 state, 0x10 timestamps, 0x18 id u32, 0x20 allocSize u64, 0x28 width u64, 0x30 height u64, 0x38 bytesPerRow u64, 0x40 offset u64, 0x48 pixelFormat u32, 0x58 planeCount, 0x60 bpe u16, 0x62/0x63 element w/h, 0x64 cacheMode, 0x70 parentID, 0x74 slices, planes at 0x88 + i*0x98 (width, height, +0x18 offset, +0x20 bytesPerRow, +0x28 size, +0x30 bpe u16, +0x32/33 element w/h); total 0xc68. Our iOS 16 layout (u32 fields at 0x1c.., 0xa68) made width/height/rowbytes garbage. 0159 writes the iOS 18 layout.
- Sysctls/syscalls triage: vm.malloc_ranges ENOENT = libmalloc range feature off, benign; lockdown_mode_state, amfi developer_mode_status, codesigning security_boot_mode_complete = closed-kext (Sandbox/AMFI) stand-ins; fcntl 63 = F_GETPROTECTIONCLASS on a non-cprotect HFS mount; clonefileat(462) and renameatx_np ENOTSUP = HFS has no clone/swap; work_interval_ctl(499) ENOTSUP; Mach traps 19/40/76 = mod_refs/get_attributes/type on dead names (kr 0x11/0xf), benign.

### 2026-10-23 black screen with frames presented = iOS 18 IOMFB swap record layout (0160)

- 5000+ swaps counted but no `SWEET FB: swap N layer ...` line ever (it is logged for the first 16 layers with a surface): every swap looked empty. IOMobileFramebuffer 22H352 sends selector 5 with a 0x514 byte record (client+0x18; iOS 16: 0x46c) and its fields moved +0x48 (read from SwapSetLayer/SwapBegin): +0x98 swap id, +0x9c+4i surface id, +0xac+16i source rect, +0x10c+16i dest rect, +0x15c+4i layer flags; no layer mask (id != 0 = in use), while the driver read mask at 0x104 and ids at 0x54. 0160 translates the record into the legacy layout in the SwapEnd handler.

**USB correction (2026-10-20):** the Poco F1's port is **USB 2.0 only** (no SuperSpeed lanes; fastboot enumerates at `Link Speed: 480 Mb/s` on the Mac). `DCFG.DEVSPD=4` / `DSTS` speed 4 left by ABL is the configured maximum of the USB3-capable DWC3 3.00a, NOT a SuperSpeed link: the QMP USB3 PHY is never used, ABL's gadget works at HS without it. So "init the QMP PHY" is not the fix. Tried so far (all still `ep0 cmd 0x1 timeout, DEPCMD 0x401`): soft reset (0084-0088), UTMI-as-PIPE clock (0087), no soft reset + halt (0089), GUCTL1 bit 26 + apps_smmu sid 0x740 (0094). **Build a phone image** (kernel K, ADT A): `python3 tools/mkpayload.py $K $A shim/out/payload-X.bin --cmdline "$B" && (cd shim && ./build.sh ../shim/out/payload-X.bin && cp out/shim-boot.img out/shim-boot-X.img)` then check `python3 tools/unpack_bootv2.py shim/out/shim-boot-X.img shim/out/check-X && cmp shim/out/check-X/ramdisk shim/out/payload-X.bin`. QEMU smoke test: `python3 tools/mkpayload.py $K research/q0057-hi.adt $S/csq/payloadX.bin --ramdisk $S/csq/root-fork.img --cmdline "debug=0xc serial=3 cpus=1 keepsyms=1 -enable_kprintf_spam -noprogress rd=md0"` + `PATH=~/swift/usr/bin:$PATH python3 tools/qemu-sweet.py ... --secs 40 --kernel $K --fail-on-panic` (S = scratchpad; root-fork.img lives in the session scratchpad csq/ dir and may be gone in a new session).

**Kernel workflow:** edit `research/bt28/xnu/...` (keep the pre-edit copy in `research/preNNNN/`), syntax check `python3 tools/xnu-cc.py research/run28.log research/bt28 <file>`, make `xnu/patches/NNNN-*.patch` with `diff -u pre bt28 | sed` path rewrite, add new/changed files to `research/check-patch-order.py` list, run it (must say applied + MATCH), commit + push -> GitHub Actions `xnu` builds (~8 min) -> `gh run download <id> -R dipa-studio/ios-sweet -n xnu-kernel -D xnu/out-NNNN`. **Repo is PUBLIC since 2026-10-17** (private macOS runner minutes ran out: "recent account payments have failed"); public = free runners. Commit trailer: only `Co-Authored-By: dipa-studio <183933817+dipa-studio@users.noreply.github.com>`.

**Phone workflow (user does buttons, Claude runs commands):**
- Boot test kernel: phone in fastboot -> `fastboot boot shim/out/shim-boot-X.img`. NEVER `fastboot getvar`.
- **Read logs WITHOUT filming:** after a test the user holds Power+VolDown -> fastboot -> `fastboot boot recovery/recovery.img` (OrangeFox, temp) -> wait for `adb shell true` -> `tools/sweet-log-pull.sh /run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/logs/<name>.txt` (reads the 4 MiB console ring the kernel keeps in the last 1025 blocks of userdata; patch 0047). Then `adb reboot bootloader` brings the phone back to fastboot without the user.
- **Flash a root image:** `fastboot flash -S 512M userdata <img>.simg` (7 min, 25 chunks). After a big flash the Xiaomi ABL fastboot hangs: next `fastboot boot` stalls at "Sending" -> the USER must re-enter fastboot by hand (Power+VolDown). Do not retry commands against the hung ABL. A flash right after `adb reboot bootloader` once failed on chunk 1 (USB protocol error): just retry. User must NOT unplug during flash (once interrupted at 25/25 -> reflash).
- **Edit the phone root without reflashing (same-length only):** find the file's raw offset in the local image (it is one extent; search the image for its first 4 KiB), compare with `adb exec-out dd if=/dev/block/sda34 ...`, then `adb push` changed 4 KiB blocks and `dd of=/dev/block/sda34 seek=... conv=notrunc,fsync`, read back. Recovery kernel has no hfsplus.
- **Pull the whole phone root** (12 min): `adb exec-out "dd if=/dev/block/sda34 bs=1048576 count=16384" > img`; mount ro with udisksctl or `sudo mount -t hfsplus -o loop,ro`. To write into a pulled image: `sudo fsck.hfsplus -fy` first (volume is never cleanly unmounted -> Linux mounts ro even with -o force), then `sudo mount -t hfsplus -o loop,force`.
- Making images: copy latest img with `cp --sparse=always`, mount rw with force, change, `sync; umount`, `sha256sum`, `img2simg`. Keep only the latest image + one fallback on the SSD (/home is ~98% full; big work files live in `/run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/`, old kernels/images archived in `.../ios-sweet-work/archive/`).

**Diagnostics built into the kernel (all SWEET-only):** persistent console log (0047, boot-arg sweetlog=0 off; flush every ~1 s); `|name| text` copies of /dev/null writes for processes in `sweetnull` (default backboardd,SpringBoard; 0048); `IOK name:` IOKit lookup/open trace for `sweetiolog` (default backboardd,SpringBoard; 0049/0054, 400 lines max); `SWEET Sandbox: proc op N` (0050); `STR ...` heap text dump on SIGABRT/SIGTRAP for `sweetscan` (default backboardd,SpringBoard; 0051/0053) - this is how NSException/BSAssert reasons were found; `SMP name tN pc .. bt ..` user stack sampler for `sweetsample` (default SpringBoard,backboardd; first at 90 s then every 120 s, 40 samples, 16 frames; 0054/0055); `SWEET FB: N swaps` every 1000 swaps; `CRASH name ...` crash reports (0043/0044). Symbolicate cache addresses with `ipsw dyld a2s research/dsc/dyld_shared_cache_arm64 <addr - slide>`; main-binary addresses: addr - mh + 0x100000000.

**Patches added today (0046-0057, all CI-built):** 0046 IOSurface (IOCoreSurfaceRoot) + IOMobileFramebuffer (AppleCLCD on /arm-io/disp0) + arm-io nubs; 0047 persistent log; 0048 /dev/null tee; 0049 IOKit trace; 0050 permissive "Sandbox" MAC policy; 0051/0053 heap text on crash; 0052 RENAME_SWAP via exchangedata on HFS; 0054/0055 sampler, swap counter, AKS selectors 35 (extended device state = 17) and 101; 0056 panel copy from the vsync timer at 10 Hz (sweetfb_hz) instead of every swap; 0057 no config_ext_resolver (ACL group entries resolve without memberd). - **v11 still migrated (boot 0056u, 20:32 UTC, `logs/boot-0056u-v11.txt`).** On iOS kCFPreferencesCurrentHost lands in the plain plist: all DMEnvironment setters (setLastBuildVersionPref, setUserDataDispositionPref, ...) use CurrentHost and the phone's `/var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com.apple.migration.plist` holds DMUserDataDisposition; datamigrator's XPC service runs with `_RoleAccount: mobile`. **Image v12** = v11 + `LastSystemVersion` = 20H392 added to that plain `com.apple.migration.plist` (keeps DMUserDataDisposition, 501:501 0600) + `/Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/HAL/VirtualAudio.plugin` moved to `/Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/HAL.disabled/` (Codex: stops the mediaserverd SIGSEGV loop; untested). **installcoordinationd loop explained (Codex `codex/installcoordinationd-report.md`):** os_crash "Failed to create data directory" EACCES for `/private/var/containers/Shared/SystemGroup/systemgroup.com.apple.installcoordinationd/Library/InstallCoordination`: `Library`/`Library/Caches` in the container are ACL-only (uid/gid 0xfffffffe, mode 0000, ACE for group GUID ...012B = gid 299 systemusers); with `config_ext_resolver` the kernel asks an identity resolver (memberd/opendirectoryd) that iOS does not run -> EWOULDBLOCK -> ACE skipped -> EACCES. **Patch 0057**: drop config_ext_resolver from FILESYS_BASE (compatibility UID/GID<->GUID mapping is used instead), likely fixes other ACL-protected containers too. Codex task 3: UFS driver performance design (`codex/ufs-performance.md`).
- **mediaserverd SIGSEGV loop explained (Codex report `/run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/codex/mediaserverd-report.md`):** the fault (FAR 0x390) is in the HAL plug-in `/Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/HAL/VirtualAudio.plugin/VirtualAudio` (ApplyBeamFormerPreference <- VirtualAudio_PlugIn::SetPropertyData <- HALS_AHPPlugIn::ObjectSetPropertyData <- AudioObjectSetPropertyData <- MediaExperience vaemVADSerializationListener): the default virtual audio device ('vdef', VirtualAudioDevice_Default) does not exist (no audio hardware/driver, no /product/audio). Proposed workaround for GUI bring-up: move VirtualAudio.plugin out of /Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/HAL/ in the next root image (untested). v11 reflashed OK after an interrupted flash (USB unplugged at chunk 25/25). Codex task 2: installcoordinationd crash loop.
- **ROOT CAUSE of the endless migration: no IOPlatformUUID (2026-10-17).** datamigrator's `-[DMMigrator isMigrationNeededWithBuildVersion:lastBuildVersion:clientPID:]` gets lastBuildVersion from `-[DMEnvironment lastBuildVersionPref]` = `CFPreferencesCopyValue("LastSystemVersion", "com.apple.migration", kCFPreferencesCurrentUser, kCFPreferencesCurrentHost)` -> a ByHost file. The phone had `/var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com.apple.migration.plist` (only DMUserDataDisposition) and NO `Preferences/ByHost` directory at all. On arm XNU publishes IOPlatformUUID (gethostuuid) only from `/chosen/unique-chip-id` (8 bytes) + `/chosen/chip-id` (4 bytes) (IOPlatformExpertDevice::generatePlatformUUID, called from IOPlatformExpert::start); the SWEET ADT had neither, so every CurrentHost preference write failed. Fix: mkadt adds `unique-chip-id` = 0x5357454554000001 (synthetic) and `chip-id` = 0x8015 -> UUID `A9683613-3704-53F2-95D0-98A8FB574E78` (same SHA1 namespace computation as the kernel). ADT `shim/out/sweet-0057hi.adt`, boot image `shim/out/shim-boot-0056u.img` (0056 kernel + 0057hi ADT, QEMU OK). **Image v11** = v10 + `Preferences/ByHost/com.apple.migration.<UUID upper and lower>.plist` {LastSystemVersion: 20H392} (501:501, dir 0755 files 0600). Flashing v11 2026-10-17 ~20:10 UTC. Codex (gpt-6.1-sol high) analysing the mediaserverd SIGSEGV loop in `/run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/codex/` (report `mediaserverd-report.md`).
- **v10 did NOT stop the migration (boot 0056n on v10, 19:18 UTC, `logs/boot-0056n-v10.txt`):** datamigrator + migrationpluginwrapper ran again, backboardd drew the progress UI (13000+ swaps in ~5 min). So `com.apple.DataMigration.plist`/LastSystemVersion is not the deciding input. What is known about the decision (datamigrator XPC service `DataMigration.framework/XPCServices/com.apple.datamigrator.xpc/com.apple.datamigrator`, strings): `Migration sentinel present. Migration necessary.` (sentinel `/private/var/datamigrator/migrate`, created and removed by datamigrator itself; absent in v10), `System build version changed from %@ to %@. Migration necessary.` / `...unchanged... not necessary`, `Disposition dictionary with build version %@ is out of date relative to current build version %@`, `SyntheticDidUpgrade`, DMUserDataDispositionManager; previous build source not found yet (not in the binary's plain strings; likely in DataMigration.framework's DMUserDataDispositionManager / preferences domain `com.apple.migration`). backboardd calls `DMPerformMigrationIfNeeded` and holds SpringBoard in `checkInAndWaitForDataMigration` until it returns. NEXT: find where the previous build version / disposition dictionary is stored (disassemble DataMigration.framework DMUserDataDispositionManager + datamigrator's isMigrationNeeded path), write it into the image; or get datamigrator's os_log out (OS_ACTIVITY_DT_MODE needs a launchd env change).

- **Boot 0055n (17:16 UTC, ~65 min, `logs/boot-0055n.txt`): migration bar reached 99% in ~30 min (was ~60), then crawled.** Root cause of the slowness: backboardd presents the progress UI at ~58 Hz (**167,000 swaps in ~48 min**) and AppleCLCD copied the full 10 MB frame into the panel on every swap -> ~600 MB/s memcpy on the single CPU (cpus=1), starving everything (polled UFS I/O too). Sampler (16 frames): **SpringBoard waits in `-[BKSSystemShellService _checkIn]`** (synchronous BSXPC call to backboardd, from FBSystemShellInitialize <- SBSystemAppMain) - backboardd holds the system shell check-in until the data migration ends, so the migration is the gate to SpringBoard UI. **Patch 0056**: swap only records the latest swap record; the vsync timer copies it to the panel every 60/sweetfb_hz ticks (default 10 Hz); `SWEET FB: N swaps` every 1000.
- **Boot 0054n (16:53 UTC, ~12 min, `logs/boot-0054n.txt`, patch 0054 sampler):** the data migration runs AGAIN on every boot (it completed once in the 0053s run) - completion is not being recorded. backboardd keeps swapping the progress UI (3700+ swaps in ~10 min, `SWEET FB: N swaps`). Sample during migration: SpringBoard main thread blocked in a synchronous XPC call (`mach_msg2_trap <- dispatch_mach_send_and_wait_for_reply <- xpc_connection_send_message_with_reply_sync <- -[BSXPCServiceConnectionMessage _sendSynchronously:] <- BSXPCServiceConnectionProxy ...`). Unsupported AKS selectors seen: 101 `aks_assert_consume`, 93 `aks_get_internal_info`, 34 `_aks_stash_load` (datamigrator), **35 `aks_get_extended_device_state`** (SpringBoard, chronod, remindd, searchd, symptomsd...). **Patch 0055**: AKS 35 answered like 17 (same DER device state), 101 succeeds; sampler backtraces 16 frames, 40 samples (~80 min).
- **MILESTONE: FIRST iOS PIXELS ON THE PANEL (boot 0053s long run, 15:33 UTC, ~60 min; photos IMG_20261017_183029/183222/183419; log `/run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/logs/boot-0053s-long.txt`, 407 KB, 1428 flushes).** The iOS data-migration boot screen (Apple logo + progress bar) was shown and the bar advanced to full in ~60 minutes. It is drawn by **backboardd through our kernel display path**: `SWEET SURF: backboardd create id 2/3 1080x2400 fmt 0x42475241 (BGRA) bpe 4 bpr 4352 size 10452992 keys 11`, IOSurface sels 9, 27, 5, 34 and traps 2/3 (lock/unlock), IOMFB SwapBegin (4) / SwapEnd (5, 1132-byte struct) / GetLayerDefaultSurface (3) / 68 / 55, then `SWEET FB: swap N layer 0 surface 2|3 1080x2400 ... a 0,0 1080x2400 b 0,0 1080x2400` alternating (double buffering; only the first 16 swaps are logged). Triggered by `com.apple.datamigrator` (AKS sels 101/93/34 return unsupported) and `com.apple.migrationpluginwrapper`. Probably ProgressUI-style CoreGraphics drawing into IOSurfaces (no GPU used). After the migration: SpringBoard and backboardd stay up (exec once each, no crash), nothing new drawn; crash loops only for mediaserverd, installcoordinationd, mobileactivationd (~108 each in the hour); bluetoothd and fairplayd.H2 relaunch constantly (no hardware). Kernel console text overlays the image (sweetfb_noconsole=1 would hide it).

ADT `shim/out/sweet-0054hi.adt` (QEMU `research/q0054-hi.adt`), image `shim/out/shim-boot-0053s.img` = 0053 kernel + 0054hi ADT. Possible catch: MobileGestalt may have cached scale 0 in `/private/var/containers/Shared/SystemGroup/systemgroup.com.apple.mobilegestaltcache/...`.
- **MILESTONE: backboardd survives and opens the display (boot 0052n on v9, 15:07 UTC, `logs/boot-0052n.txt`).** No `CRASH backboardd`. It walks `IODeviceTree:/arm-io` children, matches IOMobileFramebuffer, `open AppleCLCD type 0 -> 0x0`, calls IOMFB selectors 57, 15, 22, 29, 33, 79 (GetBlock, out 1580/40), 74 (SupportedFrameInfo), 12, 6, 27, 17, 84, 50; opens IOCoreSurfaceRoot (sel 13); looks for AGXAccelerator / IOAcceleratorES / IOAcceleratorESLegacy (none, repeatedly), `IODeviceTree:/product` (none, repeatedly), `/buttons`, aop-smart-cover, IOPMrootDomain. No swaps yet. **SpringBoard now gets past display discovery** and dies (sig 5) in `-[FBSDisplayMode _initWithWidth:height:preferredScale:scaleOverride:refreshRate:gamut:hdr:rotation:virtual:validityCheck:]+580` <- `_initWithCADisplayMode:scale:rotation:` <- `FBSDisplayConfiguration _initWithImmutableDisplay:` <- `FBSDisplaySource initWithDisplay:` <- `FBSDisplayMonitor` (slide 0x14504000): "FBSDisplayMode cannot be configured as requested". Checks: width/height >= 2, preferredScale > 0, scaleOverride >= 0, refreshRate non-zero, valid gamut/HDR/rotation. Suspects: refresh rate 0 (IOMFB timing blocks answered with zeros) and/or scale 0 (MobileGestalt main-screen-scale; no /product node in the ADT). **Patch 0053**: heap text scan also on SIGTRAP and for SpringBoard (default sweetscan list backboardd,SpringBoard) to read the exact mode description.

- **Phone root pulled (16 GiB, 12 min, `adb exec-out dd if=/dev/block/sda34`) to `/run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/phone-root.img` (loop-mounted ro at /run/media/dipa/iOSRoot).** No .ips crash reports. logd tracev3 (Persist, LZ4 `bv41` blocks, decompressed strings in `ios-sweet-work/tracev3/all.txt`): /private/var/db/uuidtext is EMPTY so only launchd's own messages are readable. Findings: v8 launch failure = `Service could not initialize: posix_spawn(/usr/libexec/backboardd), error 0xd - Permission denied` (xpcproxy exit 78) - /dev/console not openable for the mobile user; backboardd[98] spawned `com.apple.MTLCompilerService` before SIGABRT; **launchd denies many mach lookups with `error = 159: Sandbox restriction`** (mediaserverd -> com.apple.backboard.display.services, remoted, mobiletimerd -> sleepd, identityservicesd -> apsd) and **tccd exits OS_REASON_SANDBOX "profile not found"** - no Sandbox kext/profiles on SWEET; likely cause of many daemon crash loops (next wall after the display). CF's uncaught-exception text never reaches stderr for daemons (no CF handler; libc++abi prints only the type). **Patch 0049** (`xnu/patches/0049-sweet-iokit-trace.patch`): `IOK name: ...` console lines for registry path lookups, child iterators, matching (with result count/class), matching notifications and IOServiceOpen of the processes in boot-arg `sweetiolog` (default backboardd), max 400 lines.
- **CONFIRMED: launchd StandardOut/ErrorPath=/dev/console stops backboardd from launching.** After the in-place rename (boot 0047n-b, log `logs/boot-0047n-b.txt`) backboardd launches again and dies as on v7: sig 6, `___pthread_kill <- abort <- demangling_terminate_handler <- _objc_terminate <- std::terminate <- _dispatch_client_callout <- _dispatch_once_callout <- main+0x9334` (window-server dispatch_once). It never opened AppleCLCD or IOCoreSurfaceRoot (no `SWEET FB/SURF: backboardd` lines) -> throws before/at display detection. **Patch 0048** (`xnu/patches/0048-sweet-devnull-tee.patch`, CI `39037749668` -> `xnu/out-0048`, image `shim/out/shim-boot-0048n.img`): writes to /dev/null by processes named in boot-arg `sweetnull` (default `backboardd,SpringBoard`) are printed as `|name| text`. Boot 0048n (13:48 UTC, log `logs/boot-0048n.txt`): only `|backboardd| libc++abi: terminating due to uncaught exception of type NSException` (CF's reason line goes to os_log, not stderr, despite CFLOG_FORCE_STDERR/OS_ACTIVITY_DT_MODE). Next: pull the whole 16 GiB root from recovery (`adb exec-out dd if=/dev/block/sda34`) to `/run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/phone-root.img` and read ReportCrash .ips files / logd tracev3 for the exception reason.

Suspect the v8 plist (StandardOut/ErrorPath=/dev/console on backboardd). **In-place plist edit from recovery (no reflash):** launchd.plist is one extent at image/partition byte offset 0x1e025f000 (195 blocks; verified bytes identical on phone vs local v8 mount); renamed StandardOutPath->StandardOutPatX and StandardErrorPath->StandardErrorPatX (same length, both jobs) in blocks 68 and 105 via `adb push` + `dd of=/dev/block/sda34 seek=... conv=notrunc,fsync`, read back VERIFIED. Phone now differs from local v8 image in those 2 blocks (files in `/run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/`). Test boot 0047n 13:38:42 UTC. Recovery kernel has no hfsplus (raw block edits only).
